# Cited Results: GHL2025

Primary paper:
Nikita Guseynov, Xiajie Huang, Nana Liu, "Quantum framework for simulating
linear PDEs with Robin boundary conditions", arXiv:2506.20478.

This ledger separates what the paper cites or treats as standard from what QBE
has actually formalized.  A dependency is not considered proved in QBE unless
the `QBE status` column says `formalized` and names a build-tested Lean
declaration.

## 2026-05-24 O_D^BS Source-Audit Correction

The O_D^BS blocker from the current row-dependent Lean model is reclassified.
The compiled collision witness remains useful, but it is a rejected-model
regression test, not a paper-level source gap.

The faithful source interpretation is:

- GHL2025 Lemma `Diagonal sparsity` and Lemma `Banded-sparse-access-oracle`
  use a global band/diagonal slot $s$ with
  $r_{si}=r_{s0}+i \bmod 2^n$.
- Remark `sparsity maximum` says the Robin heat example has $\kappa=7$
  diagonal slots, including boundary-effect diagonals.
- The one-term Robin paragraph before Theorem `1 term robin` says zeros can be
  included and then uses the full range $s=0,\dots,\kappa-1$.

Therefore zero-amplitude boundary entries should be represented as zero
coefficients in the amplitude layer, while the sparse-access index oracle keeps
the global slot.  The active address and source-domain transcript now use
`oneTermRobinGlobalSparseAddress` and
`bandedSparseAccessPaperGlobalSlotSource`.  Historical notes below that named
`QBE.ODBS.UnusedZeroBranchExtension` as the active blocker are superseded:
that row is retained only as rejected-model memory for the old row-dependent
helper.  The current proof blockers are the corrected global-source
permutation, inverse-on-range, dagger cleanup, unitarity, and final block
extraction obligations.

## 2026-05-24 Middle Global-Source Cleanup Wrapper

No new external result is accepted in this middle packet.  The compiled theorem
`bandedSparseAccessPostSwapCleanup_of_globalSlotSourceCandidate_noRange` is a
Lean-local bridge from the active global-source predicate to the existing
conditional post-SWAP cleanup candidate.  It reuses the audited GHL2025 Lemma
`Banded-sparse-access-oracle`, the zero-inclusion paragraph before Theorem
`1 term robin`, and the corrected global-slot address contract recorded in
`QBE.ODBS.GlobalSparseSlotAddress`.

The theorem is not an inverse-on-range uniqueness proof and does not change
`daggerCleanup.proved`, either O_D^BS unitarity flag, LCU correctness, or final
block extraction.

## 2026-05-24 Middle Cleanup-Scope Domain Decision

No new external result is accepted in this middle packet.  The cleanup-scope
decision remains internal proof-state bookkeeping over the audited GHL2025
Lemma `Banded-sparse-access-oracle`, Fig. `1 term ROBIN`, and the corrected
global sparse-slot contract in `QBE.ODBS.GlobalSparseSlotAddress`.

The next lower packet may add only a narrow active global-source interface that
packages `oneTermRobinBlockEncodingProofRoute_odbsRestrictedDaggerColumnIndicator`
with `oneTermRobinBlockEncodingProofRoute_odbsCleanupScopeDecision`.  This use

semantic cleanup, unitary extension, LCU correctness, block projection, and
block correctness false.

Any attempt to promote full clean-domain cleanup remains a source-contract
obligation unless QBE records an exact image rule for every clean branch.  Any
attempt to promote full-space reversible extension or O_D^BS unitarity must
first update the cited-results row for the prior PDE sparse-access construction
or add a new precise QBE reversible-extension theorem target.  The historical
row-dependent collision remains `contract-drift` memory only.

## Cycle 10 O_D^BS Source-Domain Audit Note

The source audit used arXiv:2506.20478v2.  Lemma
`Banded-sparse-access-oracle and resource cost` states the padded-register map
$|0\rangle^{n-l}|s\rangle^l|i\rangle^n \mapsto |r_{si}\rangle^n|i\rangle^n$.
The Robin one-term construction then says zeros can be included in the set of
nonzero elements and uses diagonal sparsity $\kappa$; Eq. `ROBIN clarified`
sums over $s=0,\dots,\kappa-1$.  Therefore
`bandedSparseAccessPaperValidCleanSource` is only a classifier for nonzero
stencil branches.  It is not sufficient as the faithful source domain for
`O_D^BS` unless paired with a separate contract explaining the zero-amplitude
branches.

The next required QBE artifact is an unused-branch extension contract.  Until
that contract exists and is proved or explicitly kept as an obligation,
`GHL2025.Lemma1.ODBS` remains `contract-only`; injectivity, dagger cleanup, and
unitary extension remain obligations.

## 2026-05-26 Boundary `R_y` Corrected-Angle Source Audit

The row `GHL2025.RyBoundary.AngleConventionBoundarySlot2` is updated from a
blocking source-contract gap to a source-backed correction route.  The local
GHL2025 line Eq. `eq:angles for Ry` writes
`theta_j^s = arccos(D_j^(s) / N_D)`, but the standard `R_y(theta)` convention
used by the companion implementation has clean entry `cos(theta/2)`.  The
source-backed continuation is therefore:

$$
theta_j^s = 2 arccos(D_j^(s) / N_D).
$$
